pratiṣṭhāÙ śatadhā hi |

[[2-9-1]]

[[2-10-1]]
paÈca vā ete mahāyajÈāssatati pratāyante satati santiṣṭhante devayajÈaḥ pitṛyajÈo bhūtayajÈo manuṣyayajÈo brahmayajÈa iti yadagnau juhotyapi samidhaṃ taddevayajÈassantiṣṭhate yatpitṛbhyassvadhākarotyapyapastatpitṛyajÈassantiṣṭhate yadbhūtebhyo baliÙ harati tadbhūtayajÈassantiṣṭhate yadbrāhmaṇebhyo'nnaṃ dadāti tanmanuṣyayajÈassantiṣṭhate yatsvādhyāyamadhīyītaikāmapyṛcaṃ yajussāma vā tadbrahmayajÈassantiṣṭhate yadṛco'dhīte payasaḥ kūlyā asya pitÅnthsvadhā abhivahanti yadyajūÙṣi ghṛtasya kūlyā yatsāmāni soma ebhyaḥ pavate yadatharvāṅgiraso madhoḥ kūlyā yadbrāhmaṇānītihāsānpurāṇāni kalpāngāthā nārāśaÙsīrmedasaḥ kūlyā asya pitÅnthsvadhā abhivahanti yadṛco'dhīte paya āhutibhireva taddevāÙstarpayati yadyajūÙṣi ghṛtāhutibhiryatsāmāni somāhutibhiryadatharvāṅgiraso madhvāhutibhiryadbrāhmaṇānītihāsānpurāṇāni kalpāngāthā nārāśaÙsīrmedāhutitibhireva taddevāÙstarpayati ta enaṃ tṛptā āyuṣā tejasā varcasā śriyā yaśasā brahmavarcasenānnādyena ca tarpayanti || 14 || || 10 ||

brahmayajÈena yakṣyamāṇaḥ prācyāṃ diśi grāmādachadirdarśa udīcyāṃ prāgudīcyāṃ vodita āditye dakṣiṇata upavīyopaviśya hastāvavanijya trirācāmeddviḥ parimṛjya sakṛdupaspṛśya śiraścakṣuṣī nāsike śrotre hṛdayamālabhya yattrirācāmati tena ṛcaḥ prīṇāti yaddviḥ parimṛjati tena yajuÙṣi yatsakṛdupaspṛśati tena sāmāni yatsavyaṃ pāṇiṃ pādau prokṣati yacchiraścakṣuṣī nāsike śrotre hṛdayamālabhate tenāthavāṅgiraso brāhmaṇānītihāsānpurāṇāni kalpāngāthā nārāśaÙsīḥ prīṇāti darbhāṇāṃ mahadupastīryopasthaṃ kṛtvā prāṅāsīnassvādhyāyamadhīyītāpāṃ vā eṣa auṣadhīnāÙ raso yaddarbhāssarasameva brahma kurute dakṣiṇottarau pāṇī pādau kṛtvā sapavitrāvomiti pratipadyata etadvai yajustrayīṃ vidyāṃ pratyeṣā vāgetatparamamakṣaraṃ tadetadṛcā'bhyukta mṛco akṣare parame vyomanyasmindevā adhi viśve niṣeduryastanna veda kimṛcā kariṣyati ya ittādvidusta ime samāsata iti trīneva prāyuṅkta bhūrbhuvassvarityāhaitadvai vācassatyaṃ yadeva vācassatyaṃ tatprāyuṅktātha sāvitrīṃ gāyatrīṃ triranvāha paccho'rdharcaśo'navānaÙ savitā śriyaḥ prasavitā śriyamevāpnotyatho prajÈātayaiva pratipadā chandāÙsi pratipadyate || 15 || || 11 ||
[[2-12-1]]

ye devayānā uta pitṛyāṇāssarvānpatho anṛṇā ākṣīyemetyagniṃ vai jātaṃ pāpmā jagrāha taṃ devā āhutībhiḥ pāpmānamapāghnannāhutīnāṃ yajÈena yajÈasya dakṣiṇābhirdakṣiṇānāṃ brāhmaṇena brāhmaṇasya chandobhiśchandasāÙ svadhyāyenāpahatapāpmā svādhyāyo devapavitraṃ vā etattaṃ yo'nūthsṛjatyabhāgo vāci bhavatyabhāgo nāke tadeṣā'bhyuktā |

[[2-18-1]]

[[3-11-1]]
suvarnaṃ gharmaṃ pariveda venam | indrasyātmānaṃ daśadhā carantam | antassamudre manasā carantam | brahmā'nvavindaddaśahotāramarṇe | antaḥ praviṣṭaśśāstā janānām | ekassanbahudhā vicāraḥ |

[[3-11-2]]

tvaṣṭāraÙ rūpāṇi vikurvantaṃ vipaścim || 21 ||

yasminnidaÙ saṃ ca vi caiti sarvaṃ yasmindevā adhi viśve niṣeduḥ | tadeva bhūtaṃ tadu bhavyamā idaṃ tadakṣare parame vyomann | yenāvṛtaṃ khaṃ ca divaṃ mahīṃ ca yenādityastapati tejasā bhrājasā ca | yamantassamudre kavayo vayanti yadakṣare parame prajāḥ |
yataḥ prasūtā jagataḥ prasūtī toyena jīvānvyacasarja bhūmyām | yadoṣadhībhiḥ puruṣānpaśūÙśca viveśa bhūtāni carācarāṇi |
ataḥ paraṃ nānyadaṇīyasaÙ hi parātparaṃ yanmahato mahāntam | yadekamavyaktamanantarūpaṃ viśvaṃ purāṇaṃ tamasaḥ parastāt || 1 ||
[[10-1-2]]
tadevartaṃ tadu satyamāhustadeva brahma paramaṃ kavīnām | iṣṭāpūrtaṃ bahudhā jātaṃ jāyamānaṃ viśvaṃ bibharti bhuvanasya nābhiḥ | tadevāgnistadvāyustathsūryastadu candramāḥ |
tadeva śukramamṛtaṃ tadbrahma tadāpassa prajāpatiḥ |
sarve nimeṣā jajÈire vidyutaḥ puruṣādadhi |
kalā muhūrtāḥ kāṣṭhāścāhorātrāśca sarvaśaḥ |
ardhamāsā māsā ṛtavassaṃvathsaraśca kalpantām |
sa āpaḥ pradughe ubhe ime antarikṣamatho suvaḥ |
nainamūrdhvaṃ na tiryaÈcaṃ na madhye parijagrabhat |
na tasyeśe kaścana tasya nāma mahadyaśaḥ || 2 ||
[[10-1-3]]
na saṃdṛśe tiṣṭhati rūpamasya na cakṣuṣā paśyati kaścanainam |
hṛdā manīṣā manasā'bhiklṛpto ya enaṃ viduramṛtāste bhavanti | adbhyassaṃbhūto hiraṇyagarbha ityaṣṭau |
eṣa hi devaḥ pradiśo'nu sarvāḥ pūrvo hi jātassa u garbhe antaḥ |
sa vijāyamānassa janiṣyamāṇaḥ pratyaṅmukhāstiṣṭhati viśvatomukhaḥ | viśvataścakṣuruta viśvatomukho viśvatohasta uta viśvataspāt |
saṃ bāhubhyāṃ namati saṃ patatrairdyāvāpṛthivī janayandeva ekaḥ | venastatpaśyanviśvā bhuvanāni vidvānyatra viśvaṃ bhavatyekanīÒam | yasminnidaÙ saṃ ca vi caikaÙ sa otaḥ protaśca vibhuḥ prajāsu |
pra tadvoce amṛtaṃ nu vidvāngandharvo nāma nihitaṃ guhāsu || 3 ||
[[10-1-4]]
trīṇi padā nihitā guhāsu yastadveda savituḥ pitā sat |

pari dyāvāpṛthivī yanti sadyaḥ pari lokānpari diśaḥ pari suvaḥ | ṛtasya tantuṃ vitataṃ vicṛtya tadapaśyattadabhavatprajāsu | parītya lokānparītya bhūtāni parītya sarvāḥ pradiśo diśaśca | prajāpatiḥ prathamajā ṛtasyātmanā''tmānamabhisaṃbabhūva | sadasaspatimadbhutaṃ priyamindrasya kāmyam |
saniṃ medhāmayāsiṣam |
uddīpyasva jātavedo'paghnannirṛtiṃ mama || 4 ||
[[10-1-5]]
paśūÙśca mahyamāvaha jīvanaṃ ca diśo diśa | mā no hiÙsījjātavedo gāmaśvaṃ puruṣaṃ jagat |

satyaṃ paraṃ paraÙ satyaÙ satyena na suvargāllokāccyavante kadācana satāÙ hi satyaṃ tasmāthsatye ramante tapa iti tapo nānaśanātparaṃ yaddhi paraṃ tapastaddurdharṣaṃ taddurādharṣaṃ tasmāttapasi ramante dama iti niyataṃ brahmacāriṇastasmāddame ramante śama ityaraṇye munayastamācchame ramante dānamiti sarvāṇi bhūtāni praśaÙsanti dānānnātiduścaraṃ tasmāddāne ramante dharma iti dharmeṇa sarvamidaṃ parigṛhītaṃ dharmānnātiduṣkaraṃ tasmāddharme ramante prajana iti bhūyāÙsastasmādbhūyiṣṭhāḥ prajāyante tasmādbhūyiṣṭhāḥ prajanane ramante'gnaya ityāha tasmādagnaya ādhātavyāḥ agnihotramityāha tasmādagnihotre ramante yajÈa iti yajÈo hi devāstasmādyajÈe ramante mānasamiti vidvāÙsastasmādvidvāÙsa eva mānase ramante nyāsa iti brahmā brahmā hi paraḥ paro hi brahmā tāni vā etānyavarāṇi parāÙsi nyāsa evātyarecayadya evaṃ vedetyupaniṣat || 99 || || 78 ||

prājāpatyo hāruṇissuparṇeyaḥ prajāpatiṃ pitaramupasasāra kiṃ bhagavantaḥ paramaṃ vadantīti tasmai provāca satyena vāyurāvāti satyenādityo rocate divi

satyaṃ vācaḥ pratiṣṭhā satye sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ tasmāthsatyaṃ paramaṃ vadanti tapasā devā devatāmagra āyantapasarṣayassuvaranvavindantapasā sapatnānpraṇudāmārātīstapasi sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ tasmāttapaḥ paramaṃ vadanti damena dāntāḥ kilbiṣamavadhūnvanti damena brahmacāriṇassuvaragacchandamo bhūtānāṃ durādharṣaṃ dame sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ tasmāddamaḥ paramaṃ vadanti śamena śāntāśśivamācaranti śamena nākaṃ munayo'nvavindaÈcchamo bhūtānāṃ durādharṣaÈchame sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ tasmācchamaḥ paramaṃ vadanti dānaṃ yajÈānāṃ varūthaṃ dakṣiṇā loke dātāraÙ sarvabhūtānyupajīvanti dānenārātīrapānudanta dānena dviṣanto mitrā bhavanti dāne sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ tasmāddānaṃ paramaṃ vadanti dharmo viśvasya jagataḥ pratiṣṭhā loke dharmiṣṭhaṃ prajā upasarpanti dharmeṇa pāpamapanudati dharme sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ tasmāddharmaṃ paramaṃ vadanti prajananaṃ vai pratiṣṭhā loke sādhu prajāyāstantuṃ tanvānaḥ pitṛṇāmanṛṇo bhavati tadeva tasyā anṛṇaṃ tasmātprajananaṃ paramaṃ vadantyagnayo vai trayī vidyā devayānaḥ panthā gārhapatya ṛkpṛthivī rathantaramanvāhāryapacanaṃ yajurantarikṣaṃ vāmadevyamāhavanīyassāma suvargo loko bṛhattasmādagnīnparamaṃ vadantyagnihotraÙ sāyaṃ prātargṛhāṇāṃ niṣkṛtissviṣṭaÙ suhutaṃ yajÈakratūnāṃ prāyaṇaÙ suvargasya lokasya jyotistasmādagnihotraṃ paramaṃ vadanti yajÈa iti yajÈena hi devā divaṃ gatā yajÈenāsurānapānudanta yajÈena dviṣanto mitrā bhavanti yajÈe sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ tasmādyajÈaṃ paramaṃ vadanti mānasaṃ vai prājāpatyaṃ pavitraṃ mānasena manasā sādhu paśyati mānasā ṛṣayaḥ prajā asṛjanta mānase sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ tasmānmānasaṃ paramaṃ vadanti nyāsa ityāhurmanīṣiṇo brahmāṇaṃ brahmā viśvaḥ katamassvayaṃbhuḥ prajāpatissaṃvathsara iti saṃvathsaro'sāvādityo ya eṣa āditye puruṣassa parameṣṭhī brahmātmā yābhirādityastapati raśmibhistābhiḥ parjanyo varṣati parjanyenauṣadhivanaspatayaḥ prajāyanta oṣadhivanaspatibhirannaṃ bhavatyannena prāṇāḥ prāṇairbalaṃ balena tapastapasā śraddhā śraddhayā medhā medhayā manīṣā manīṣayā mano manasā śāntiśśāntyā cittaṃ cittena smṛti× smṛtyā smāra× smāreṇa vijÈānaṃ vijÈānenātmānaṃ vedayati tasmādannaṃ dadanthsarvāṇyetāni dadātyannātprāṇā bhavanti bhūtānāṃ prāṇairmano manasaśca vijÈānaṃ vijÈānādānando brahma yonissa vā eṣa puruṣaḥ paÈcadhā paÈcātmā yena sarvamidaṃ protaṃ pṛthivī cāntarikṣaṃ ca dyauśca diśaścāvāntaradiśāśca sa vai sarvamidaṃ jagatsa sa bhūtaÙ sa bhavyaṃ jijÈāsaklṛpta ṛtajā rayiṣṭhāśśraddhā satyo mahasvāntapasovariṣṭhādjÈātvā tamevaṃ manasā hṛdā ca bhūyo na mṛtyumupayāhi vidvāntasmānnyāsameṣāṃ tapasāmatiriktamāhuḥ vasuraṇvo vyāhṛtirahutiryadasya vijÈānaṃ tajjuhoti yathsāyaṃ prātaratti tathsamidhaṃ yatprātarmadhyaṃdinaÙ sāyaṃ ca tāni savanāni ye ahorātre te darśapūrṇamāsau ye'rddhamāsāśca māsāśca te cāturmāsyāni ya ṛtavaste paśubandhā ye saṃvathsarāśca parivathsarāśca te'hargaṇāssarvavedasaṃ vā etathsatraṃ yanmaraṇaṃ tadavabhṛtha etadvai jarāmaryamagnihotraÙ satraṃ ya evaṃ vidvānudagayane pramīyate devānāmeva mahimānaṃ gatvā''dityasya sāyujyaṃ gacchatyatha yo dakṣiṇe pramīyate pitṛṇāmeva mahimānaṃ gatvā candramasaḥ sāyujyaÙ salokatāmāpnotyetau vai sūryācandramasormahimānau brāhmaṇo vidvānabhijayati tasmādbrahmaṇo mahimānamāpnoti tasmādbrahmaṇo mahimānamityupaniṣat || 101 || || 80 ||
ambhasyekapaÈcāśaduttaraśataṃ jātavedase caturdaśa bhūrannaṃ bhūragnaye bhūragnaye caikamekaṃ pāhi pāhi catvāri catvāri yaśchandasāṃ dve namo brahmaṇa ṛtaṃ tapo yathā vṛkṣasyaikamekamaṇoraṇīyāÙ ścatustriÙśatsahasraśīrṣaÙ ṣaËviÙśatirādityo vā eṣa ādityo vai teja ekamekaṃ nidhanapataye trayoviÙśatissadyojātaṃ trīṇi vāmadevāyaikamaghorebhyastatpuruṣāya dve dve īśāno namo hiraṇyabāhava ekamekamṛtaÙ satyaṃ dve sarvo vai catvāri kadrudrāya trīṇi yasya vaikaṅkatī kṛṇuṣva pājo'ditirāpo vā idamekamekamāpaḥ punantu catvāryagniśca sūryaśca nava navomiti catvāryāyātu paÈcaujo'si daśottame catvāri ghṛṇistrīṇi brahmametu māṃ yāste brahmahatyāṃ dvādaśa brahma medhayā'dyā na imaṃ bhrūṇahatyāṃ brahma medhavā brahmā devānāmidaṃ vīrahatyāmekānna viÙśatirekānna viÙśatirmedhā devī medhāṃ ma indraścatvāri catvāryā māṃ medhā dve mayi medhāmekamapaitu paraṃ vātaṃ prāṇamamutrabhūyāddhariÙ śalkairagniṃ mā chido mṛtyo mā no mahāntaṃ mā nastoke prajāpate svastidā tryambakaṃ ye te sahasramayutaṃ dve dve mṛtyave svāhaikaṃ devakṛtasyaikādaśa yadvo devāḥ kāmo'kārṣīnmanyurakārṣīṃdve dve tilāÈjuhomi gāvaśśriyaṃ prajāḥ paÈca tilāḥ kṛṣṇāścorasya śrīḥ prajÈātu jātavedassapta prāṇavāktvakchira uttiṣṭha paÈca pṛthivīśabdamanovāgvyaktātmā'ntarātmā paramātmā me kṣudhe'nnamaya paÈcadaśāgnaye svāhaikacatvāriÙśardontadbrahma nava śraddhāyāṃ prāṇe niviṣṭo'mṛtaṃ juhomi caturviÙśatiśśraddhāyāṃ prāṇe niviśyāmṛtaÙ hutandaśāṅguṣṭhamātraḥ puruṣo dve vāṅma āsannaṣṭau vayassuparṇāḥ prāṇānāṃ granthirasi dve dve namo rudrāyaikaṃ tvamagne dyubhirdve śivena me santiṣṭhasva satyaṃ prājāpatyastasyaivamekamekamaśītiḥ || 80 ||
